{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-5-5","id":"msg_011CgMiVaTsxKHVYXyXqnVec","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Frequencies.md ===\n# FEATURE: Frequencies\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | List the most frequent non-null values of one column with --freq, ordered by count then byte value. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-NullTrim.md, FEATURE-CliOptions.md |\n| Provides    | freq.Top |\n| Consumes    | reader.Trim, reader.IsNull, --freq, --top |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nWith `--freq <name>`, csvstat lists the most frequent values of the named column instead of\nprofiling. The command is `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. The column is found by exact, case-sensitive comparison against the trimmed header names.\n2. Each value is trimmed before counting. Null values (empty, `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` ignoring case)\n   are not listed and not counted.\n3. One row is produced per distinct non-null value, with its count.\n4. Rows are ordered by count descending, then by value ascending by byte value.\n5. At most `--top` rows are returned. `--top` defaults to 5 and is a positive integer.\n6. A column with no non-null values produces only the header row.\n7. A column name absent from the header exits 3 with nothing on standard output and one diagnostic\n   on standard error.\n\n## Output\n\nHeader `value,count`, then the ranked rows. Serialization is owned by FEATURE-CsvOutput.md; this\nfeature supplies the ordered list of (value, count) pairs as `freq.Top`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC freq-order-and-default-top ===\nIntent: Frequencies are ordered by count descending then byte value ascending, and default to five rows.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nvalues = [\"delta\"] * 4 + [\"alpha\"] * 3 + [\"charlie\"] * 3 + [\"bravo\"] * 2 + [\"echo\"] + [\"foxtrot\"] + [\"golf\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in values:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == [\"value\", \"count\"]\ncounts = Counter(values)\nranked = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:5]\nassert rows[1:] == [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ranked]\n=== END AC freq-order-and-default-top ===\n\n=== AC freq-top-limit ===\nIntent: --top limits the number of rows listed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nvalues = [\"a\"] * 5 + [\"b\"] * 4 + [\"c\"] * 3 + [\"d\"] * 2 + [\"e\"]\ntop = 2\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in values:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) == top + 1\nranked = sorted(Counter(values).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:top]\nassert rows[1:] == [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ranked]\n=== END AC freq-top-limit ===\n\n=== AC freq-trim-and-nulls ===\nIntent: Values are trimmed before counting and null spellings are not listed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncore = \"Globex\"\nraw = [core, \"  \" + core + \"  \", core + \" \", \"\", \"NA\", \"n/a\", \"NULL\", \"Other\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in raw:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nlisted = {r[0]: int(r[1]) for r in rows[1:]}\nassert listed == {core: 3, \"Other\": 1}\n=== END AC freq-trim-and-nulls ===\n\n=== AC freq-all-null-column ===\nIntent: A column with no non-null values writes only the header.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\", \"other\"])\n        w.writerow([\"\", \"x\"])\n        w.writerow([\"NULL\", \"y\"])\n        w.writerow([\" \", \"z\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows == [[\"value\", \"count\"]]\n=== END AC freq-all-null-column ===\n\n=== AC freq-absent-column ===\nIntent: A column name absent from the header exits 3 and writes nothing to standard output.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nname = \"Kind\"\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([name])\n        w.writerow([\"v\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", name.lower(), path)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 3\nassert len(out) == 0\n=== END AC freq-case-sensitive-name ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Null values are never listed.\n- The ordering tie-break is byte value, not locale collation.\n- A failing command writes nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CsvOutput.md ===\n# FEATURE: CSV Output\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | Write profile and frequency results as RFC 4180 CSV with newline line endings, only on success. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-Counts.md, FEATURE-MinMax.md, FEATURE-NumericStats.md, FEATURE-Frequencies.md, FEATURE-ColumnSelect.md |\n| Provides    | output.WriteProfile, output.WriteFreq |\n| Consumes    | profile.Counts, profile.MinMax, profile.NumericStats, freq.Top |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nSerialize the profile and frequency results to standard output.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. Profile output has the header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median`, then\n   one row per column in file order, or the single row of the column named by `--column`.\n2. Frequency output has the header `value,count`, then the ranked rows.\n3. Writing uses `encoding/csv` with `UseCRLF` false, so every line ends with `\\n`. A field\n   containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline is quoted as RFC 4180 specifies.\n4. Output is buffered and written to standard output only when the whole command succeeds. A\n   failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error\n   naming the file and, for a rejected record, its line number.\n5. Empty statistic fields are written as empty strings.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC output-profile-shape ===\nIntent: Profile output has the declared header and one row per column in file order.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncolumns = [\"zeta\", \"alpha\", \"mid\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow(columns)\n        w.writerow([\"1\", \"x\", \"true\"])\n        w.writerow([\"2\", \"y\", \"false\"])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == [\"column\", \"type\", \"rows\", \"nulls\", \"distinct\", \"min\", \"max\", \"sum\", \"mean\", \"median\"]\nassert [r[0] for r in rows[1:]] == columns\nassert all(len(r) == len(rows[0]) for r in rows)\n=== END AC output-profile-shape ===\n\n=== AC output-newline-endings ===\nIntent: Output lines end with a line feed and never a carriage return.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f, lineterminator=\"\\r\\n\")\n        w.writerow([\"a\", \"b\"])\n        w.writerow([\"1\", \"x\"])\n        w.writerow([\"2\", \"y\"])\n    code, out_profile, err = cli_runner(path)\n    code2, out_freq, err2 = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"b\", path)\nassert code == 0\nassert code2 == 0\nassert \"\\r\" not in out_profile\nassert \"\\r\" not in out_freq\nassert out_profile.endswith(\"\\n\")\nassert out_freq.endswith(\"\\n\")\n=== END AC output-newline-endings ===\n\n=== AC output-profile-quoting ===\nIntent: A profile field containing a comma or a double quote round-trips through RFC 4180 quoting.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nwith_comma = \"remote, part-time\"\nwith_quote = 'say \"hi\" now'\nfor value in (with_comma, with_quote):\n    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n        path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n        with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n            w = csv.writer(f)\n            w.writerow([\"notes\"])\n            w.writerow([value])\n        code, out, err = cli_runner(path)\n    print(out)\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    rows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\n    header = rows[0]\n    row = dict(zip(header, rows[1]))\n    assert row[\"column\"] == \"notes\"\n    assert row[\"min\"] == value\n    assert row[\"max\"] == value\n=== END AC output-profile-quoting ===\n\n=== AC output-freq-quoting ===\nIntent: A frequency value containing a comma round-trips through RFC 4180 quoting.\nRequires: executable=go; BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-Frequencies.md ===\n# FEATURE: Frequencies\n\n| Field       | Value |\n|-------------|-------|\n| Version     | 20261024 V1 |\n| Description | List the most frequent non-null values of one column with --freq, ordered by count then byte value. |\n| Depends On  | FEATURE-NullTrim.md, FEATURE-CliOptions.md |\n| Provides    | freq.Top |\n| Consumes    | reader.Trim, reader.IsNull, --freq, --top |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nWith `--freq <name>`, csvstat lists the most frequent values of the named column instead of\nprofiling. The command is `csvstat --freq <name> [--top <n>] <file>`.\n\n## Behavior\n\n1. The column is found by exact, case-sensitive comparison against the trimmed header names.\n2. Each value is trimmed before counting. Null values (empty, `NA`, `N/A`, `NULL` ignoring case)\n   are not listed and not counted.\n3. One row is produced per distinct non-null value, with its count.\n4. Rows are ordered by count descending, then by value ascending by byte value.\n5. At most `--top` rows are returned. `--top` defaults to 5 and is a positive integer.\n6. A column with no non-null values produces only the header row.\n7. A column name absent from the header exits 3 with nothing on standard output and one diagnostic\n   on standard error.\n\n## Output\n\nHeader `value,count`, then the ranked rows. Serialization is owned by FEATURE-CsvOutput.md; this\nfeature supplies the ordered list of (value, count) pairs as `freq.Top`.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC freq-order-and-default-top ===\nIntent: Frequencies are ordered by count descending then byte value ascending, and default to five rows.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nvalues = [\"delta\"] * 4 + [\"alpha\"] * 3 + [\"charlie\"] * 3 + [\"bravo\"] * 2 + [\"echo\"] + [\"foxtrot\"] + [\"golf\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in values:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert rows[0] == [\"value\", \"count\"]\ncounts = Counter(values)\nranked = sorted(counts.items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:5]\nassert rows[1:] == [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ranked]\n=== END AC freq-order-and-default-top ===\n\n=== AC freq-top-limit ===\nIntent: --top limits the number of rows listed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\nfrom collections import Counter\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\nvalues = [\"a\"] * 5 + [\"b\"] * 4 + [\"c\"] * 3 + [\"d\"] * 2 + [\"e\"]\ntop = 2\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in values:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", \"--top\", str(top), path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nassert len(rows) == top + 1\nranked = sorted(Counter(values).items(), key=lambda kv: (-kv[1], kv[0]))[:top]\nassert rows[1:] == [[v, str(c)] for v, c in ranked]\n=== END AC freq-top-limit ===\n\n=== AC freq-trim-and-nulls ===\nIntent: Values are trimmed before counting and null spellings are not listed.\nRequires: executable=go; scope=test\n\nimport csv\nimport io\nimport os\nimport tempfile\n\nfrom drydock_go import cli_runner\n\ncore = \"Globex\"\nraw = [core, \"  \" + core + \"  \", core + \" \", \"\", \"NA\", \"n/a\", \"NULL\", \"Other\"]\nwith tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as tmp:\n    path = os.path.join(tmp, \"data.csv\")\n    with open(path, \"w\", newline=\"\") as f:\n        w = csv.writer(f)\n        w.writerow([\"k\"])\n        for v in raw:\n            w.writerow([v])\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"--freq\", \"k\", path)\nprint(out)\nprint(err)\nassert code == 0\nrows = list(csv.reader(io.StringIO(out)))\nlisted = {r[0]: int(r[1]) for r in rows[1:]}\nassert listed == {core: 3, \"Other\": 1}\n=== END AC freq-trim-and-nulls ===\n\n=== AC freq-all-null-column ===\nIntent: A column with no non-null values writes only the header.\nRequires: executable=go; Profile output has the header `column,type,rows,nulls,distinct,min,max,sum,mean,median`, then\n   one row per column in file order, or the single row of the column named by `--column`.\n2. Frequency output has the header `value,count`, then the ranked rows.\n3. Writing uses `encoding/csv` with `UseCRLF` false, so every line ends with `\\n`. A field\n   containing a comma, a double quote, or a newline is quoted as RFC 4180 specifies.\n4. Output is buffered and written to standard output only when the whole command succeeds. A\n   failing command writes nothing to standard output and one diagnostic line to standard error\n   naming the file and, for a rejected record, its line number.\n5. Empty statistic fields are written as empty strings.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC output-profile-shape ===\nIntent: Profile output has the declared header and one row per column in file order.\nRequires: executable=go;
